## Coverage evidence

All 774 linked bookshelf entries captured. This is an ownership list, not a claim that Collison read or endorsed every book. [Captured source](../data/intake_progress_261023/232.txt); [source-title decisions](../data/intake_progress_261023/decisions.json); [raw captures](../data/intake_progress_261023/raw/). The raw Reddit HTML contains access wrappers; the visible Reddit comments were inspected with the web renderer. Non-book media, unnamed books and related-thread sidebar titles were not converted into nominations.
